.claude\rules\citation-discipline.md:42:6. **TIER-1-NAMED-AUTHOR-QUOTE class for inherited patterns** — when a SOTA pattern is derived from a named author's published work (book / peer-reviewed paper / conference talk), cite verbatim-quote + author-name + publication inline. The named-author cite is TIER-1 primary-source for the PATTERN (not the implementing repo); use alongside repo file:line cites for full provenance. Exemplar: `Z:/repos/deps/mattpocock/skills/README.md` (48,857★, MIT, [VERIFIED 2026-04-30 PM via curl raw GitHub]) quotes Pragmatic Programmer (Thomas & Hunt) for "rate of feedback is your speed limit" (failure-mode #3 framing), Domain-Driven-Design (Eric Evans) for ubiquitous-language pattern (#2 framing), and Extreme Programming Explained (Kent Beck) for "invest in design every day" (#4 framing). Per `.claude/projects/Z--claude-sota-installed/memory/reference_mattpocock_skills_pattern_extract_2026_04_30.md` for the full mining trace + adoption-candidate ranking. **TIER-1-NAMED-AUTHOR-QUOTE PROVISIONAL-EXCEPTION** *(round-3 framing per T3 round-2 conf=0.88 ESCALATING finding on commit `d4df352`; supersedes round-1 "full cardinal-rule-1 satisfaction" framing — physical-only sources do NOT fully satisfy committed-tree inspectability)*: physical-only printed-book/journal/conference-publication primary anchor (ISBN + page + verbatim-quote) is a PROVISIONAL-EXCEPTION to cardinal-rule-1 (CLAUDE.md L7), NOT full satisfaction. Cite class downgraded to TIER-1-NAMED-AUTHOR-QUOTE-PROVISIONAL with REQUIRED metadata: (a) ISBN/DOI + page + verbatim-quote inline; (b) verifier-identity disclosure (who-verified-when, e.g., `[VERIFIED 2026-05-04 by orchestrator against book Tip #15 verbatim text]`); (c) explicit `non-automatable: true` marker so review hooks know to defer to human sign-off; (d) **HARD-GATE ELIGIBILITY TEST** *(Round-3 amendment a per T3 R3 NEEDS-ATTENTION conf=0.82 same-class finding on commit `1b87a6e`; supersedes round-2 "MUST prefer" soft preference framing — preference is not gateable; eligibility is)*: PROVISIONAL admission is admissible ONLY when DOI landing page + accessible PDF / publisher excerpt / archived scan (license-permitting) was SEARCHED AND UNAVAILABLE. The commit body MUST document the search (which DOI/publisher/archive was queried + return shape — e.g., "searched ACM DOI 10.1145/<doi> 2026-05-05 — paywall-only, no excerpt found; searched Internet Archive — no scan; searched publisher excerpt page — none"). Mere preference is INSUFFICIENT — if any auditable surrogate exists (DOI/PDF/excerpt/scan), use it as the normative source instead of the physical-only book cite. e.g., ACM DOI `https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/<doi>` for ACM papers IS auditable when paywall-free. Secondary aggregator URLs (Wikipedia, `https://pragprog.com/tips/`, `https://martinfowler.com/bliki/<topic>.html`, `c2.com/cgi/wiki`) are VERIFICATION PROXIES only — they help re-verify the primary quote without owning the book physically, but are NOT cited as normative source. **Independent named-reviewer sign-off mandate** *(Round-3 amendment b per T3 R3 NEEDS-ATTENTION conf=0.82 same-class finding on commit `1b87a6e`; supersedes round-2 "Lower-confidence reviewer-sign-off mandate" sole-orchestrator framing — sole-orchestrator verification is no longer admissible)*: rule edits citing TIER-1-NAMED-AUTHOR-QUOTE-PROVISIONAL MUST cite a SECOND verifier-identity BEYOND the orchestrator who replayed the verbatim quote against the source — commit body MUST embed BOTH verifier-identity-1 (the orchestrator, e.g., `[VERIFIED 2026-05-05 by orchestrator against book Tip #15 verbatim text]`) AND verifier-identity-2 (the independent named reviewer, e.g., `[VERIFIED 2026-05-05 by <human-name> against same book + same page + same quote]`) with explicit `[VERIFIED yyyy-mm-dd by <name> against <source>]` markers for each. The 2-verifier requirement closes the "self-attestation only" gap that round-2 left open. **`non-automatable: true` operator-discipline mandate (manual-only; no hook enforcement YET)** *(Round-4 amendment per T3 round-4 NEEDS-ATTENTION conf=0.82 SAME-CLASS finding on commit `d07ac71`; supersedes Round-3 amendment c "BLOCKING in T3/T4/T6 verdict-shape" claim that was prose-only without hook implementation — codex GPT-5.5 BRIDGE-MODE caught the policy/enforcement drift: rule text claimed automated BLOCKING but no T3/T4/T6 hook, verdict parser, schema, or test changed. Per closed-loop-recursive-narrowing.md §Outcome A monotone-decline path, downgraded prose closes the drift while the enforcement-hook ship is queued separately)*: when a TIER-1-NAMED-AUTHOR-QUOTE-PROVISIONAL cite is shipped, the operator MUST: (i) embed `non-automatable: true` marker inline; (ii) embed explicit "no automated enforcement, human gate only" disclosure in commit body acknowledging that automated re-verification is impossible AND naming both verifier-identities (per amendment b); (iii) accept residual provenance gap explicitly. The discipline is OPERATOR-MANUAL — NO T3/T4/T6 hook currently parses `non-automatable: true` as BLOCKING. Promotion to mechanical BLOCKING is FORWARD-REF for a separate enforcement-hook ship: `tools/non_automatable_blocking_audit.py` parsing committed rule edits + emitting BLOCKING status in T3/T4/T6 verdict-shape + regression tests at `tests/test_non_automatable_blocking_audit.py`. Do NOT claim BLOCKING semantic in commit bodies until that ship lands. .claude\rules\convergence-gate.md:129:When running the 5-row bar's Row 2 (Numeric — measurable improvement), a README presenting **≥3 numeric performance claims without methodology citation** (no repro script link, no benchmark repo, no dataset name) is **auto-FAIL** — do not attempt live A/B to verify, just REJECT. This short-circuits the primary-source rule: vendor marketing numbers that aren't backed by a reproducible artifact are false-by-default until proven otherwise.
.claude\rules\convergence-gate.md:136:5. If all claims cite reproducible artifacts, proceed to full primary-source check
.claude\rules\convergence-gate.md:138:**Why auto-FAIL not just downgrade**: across multiple prior live A/B verifications of fabrication-pattern candidates, none survived verification. The prior probability of "≥3 unsourced claims yet verifiable" is empirically near-zero. Running the bench is budget-wasting ritual.
.claude\rules\convergence-gate.md:153:If a README opens with a `[!CAUTION] Scam alert` block warning of impostor domains distributing malware, that's a STRONG axis-2 self-flag — the upstream itself is signaling the candidate exists in a high-volume scam-attractor space (typically a viral-young repo where bad actors create lookalike forks). Auto-REJECT axis-2 even before counting unsourced numeric claims.
.claude\rules\convergence-gate.md:157:**Distinguishes from §Anti-pattern Row-2 fabrication-test FAIL**: that rule fires on candidate AUTHORS publishing unsourced claims (deception via omission). .claude\rules\karpathy-adapted.md:174:> **Karpathy named-author quote (TIER-1 anchor for §5 Wiki Compounding Surface)** — *"You can outsource your thinking but you can't outsource your understanding."* — Karpathy AI Engineer fireside chat 2026-05-02 per `Z:/repos/deps/claude-code-best-practice-shan/videos/claude-karpathy-ai-engineer-02-may-26.md:153 @ HEAD 64fffd53a7c6f8e2e0b1575fdd200b65cda04737 [VERIFIED 2026-05-03 via direct Read]`. Karpathy's framing at L155 explains why this matters operationally: *"information still has to make it into my brain — and I feel like I'm becoming a bottleneck of just even knowing what are we trying to build, why is it worth doing, how do I direct my agents..."*. The quote is the named-author anchor for why §5 exists: cross-iter durable surfaces (Wiki / telemetry JSONL / feedback-memory) are the operator-side instrument that lets understanding accumulate even while thinking is outsourced to LLM agents — Wiki/JSONL writes ARE the "information makes it into my brain" surface for the next session. .claude\rules\launch-discipline.md:78:- [ ] **PROBE 18 precondition check** — target resource currently bound/locked/busy? Windows: `Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort <port>` (preferred PowerShell-native) OR `netstat -ano | findstr :<port>` (legacy fallback) OR `handle.exe -p <process>` (Sysinternals file-lock). POSIX: `lsof -i :<port>` (port-bind) OR `fuser <path>` (file-lock). Mount: `mountvol <volume> /L` OR `lsblk -o NAME,MOUNTPOINT`. Service: `Get-Service <name> | Select-Object Status` BEFORE Stop-Service.

.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:14:# Reference: TIER-1-USER-DIRECTIVE user-provided 9-layer architecture document 2026-05-10 §"DIMENSION A — GitHub Discovery & Search" prescribed stack (GitHub MCP + gh CLI + OSSInsight + Star History + deps.dev + Sourcebot + ast-grep + Semgrep) — INSPIRATION basis for the source enumeration
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:19:Every SOTA-research fire (sota-researcher dispatch / repo evaluation / candidate audit / convergence verification / replacement consideration) MUST query **≥4 distinct sources** from the discovery cohort below. Single-source convergence is FRAGILE and produces false-confidence verdicts. Discovery breadth is the input-side analog of `convergence-gate.md` Axis-1 ≥3-distinct-orgs output-side requirement.
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:21:## Discovery cohort (≥4 distinct source FAMILIES required per fire)
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:23:**Distinct source definition** (per Pattern A codex T1 Fire 29a prescriptions #1+#2+#6): A "distinct source" is a distinct **backend / data corpus / provider**, NOT a tool endpoint. Multiple MCP endpoints OR CLI commands under the SAME provider count as ONE source family. Example: `mcp__github__search_repositories` + `mcp__github__search_code` + `mcp__github__list_commits` + `gh CLI` all count as ONE source (GitHub).
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:46:- **ALREADY-INSTALLED + WIRED sources** may count any time queried with relevant results
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:47:- **NOT-WIRED sources** count ONLY when queried via documented working alternate path (WebFetch / HTTP-direct / ctx_fetch_and_index) AND return relevant results
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:49:- **Multiple endpoints / sub-capabilities under one provider** count as ONE source family (GitHub family contains gh CLI + ~30+ MCP endpoints; DeepWiki family contains 3 MCP endpoints; all sub-capabilities collapse to ONE count)
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:56:1. **List discovery sources used** explicitly in the fire's tracker.md or close-synthesis.md (e.g., "Discovery sources: GitHub MCP / Exa MCP / DeepWiki MCP / Perplexity MCP / Brave Search via WebFetch")
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:57:2. **Verify ≥4 distinct sources** were queried before any candidate ranking/verdict
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:58:3. **Score discovery breadth in SRA verdict** (forward-ref to SRA D11 when codified): ≥4 sources = PASS / <4 sources = DOWNGRADE-WITH-DISCLOSURE
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:59:4. **Document HONEST-NON-FINDING** if a source returned no relevant results — that IS the deliverable; do NOT silently drop the source
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:60:5. **Per-source result-trust hierarchy** (forward-ref to IMP-K source-quality scoring when codified — Fire 29b candidate):
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:62:   - Discovery breadth COUNT × source QUALITY = effective breadth signal (not just raw count)
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:70:- Snyk Advisor's 4-category scoring smooths popularity-bias that single-source counts amplify
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:73:Single-source narrow-grep produces:
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:80:- **Targeted re-verification of a NAMED candidate** (e.g., "verify gitnexus@1.6.4-rc.112 still maintains polyforge-noncommercial license") — single-source GitHub MCP query suffices because the target is named, not discovered
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:90:- **Star count as sufficient signal** — refuted by user-doc + Fire 28 IMP-K (source-quality > raw count); also refuted by `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/convergence-gate.md` Axis 3 fresh-paint anti-pattern
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:91:- **Source-count without verifying RELEVANT results** — running 4 different MCP queries that all return "no results" does NOT satisfy this gate; ≥4 sources MUST each return at least ONE relevant candidate (or HONEST-NON-FINDING explicit)
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:92:- **Skip the ≥4 gate because "I know the candidate already"** — refuted by Fire 27-C mem0 retro: orchestrator pre-audit ASSUMED Fire 27-B sqlite-vec finding might apply to mem0 (single-source assumption); codex T1 REFUTED; per-fire ≥4 verification mandatory regardless of prior-fire confidence
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:93:- **Treating ALREADY-INSTALLED candidates as "no need to research"** — refuted by Wave 144 Fire 1 astral-sh/uv RECLASSIFY: candidate was ALREADY-INSTALLED-VIA-SYSTEM-PATH but manifest stale; periodic re-discovery via multi-source gate catches drift
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:94:- **Counting multiple endpoints from one provider as multiple distinct sources** (per Pattern A codex T1 Fire 29a prescription #6) — refuted by §Discovery cohort "Distinct source definition" + §Counting rules: a single provider (GitHub / DeepWiki / Exa / Context7 / repomix) collapses ALL its sub-capabilities into ONE source-family count. Example anti-pattern: querying `mcp__github__search_repositories` + `mcp__github__search_code` + `mcp__github__list_commits` + `gh CLI repo view` and claiming "4 sources" — actual count = 1 source (GitHub family). Must query ≥4 DIFFERENT provider families.
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:95:- **Counting INSTALLED-NOT-WIRED sources as available without verifying alternate path** (per Pattern A codex T1 Fire 29a prescription #4+#5) — refuted by §Counting rules: NOT-WIRED sources require documented working alternate path (WebFetch / HTTP-direct / ctx_fetch_and_index) AND relevant-results return. Example anti-pattern: Perplexity@0.8.4 npm-global is INSTALLED but NOT in current .mcp.json; cannot count as "MCP source" until either wired OR queried via WebFetch fallback with relevant results.

# - Sibling env-var pattern: .claude/hooks/scripts/context_window_guard.py:28-29 @ HEAD 6e4a5f6
def _int_env(name: str, default: int) -> int:
    raw = os.getenv(name)
    if raw is None or raw == "":
        return default
    try:
        return int(raw)
    except ValueError:
        return default

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""SessionStart compact-hint reader (W173 P1(a) recompose).

Fires ONLY on SessionStart matcher=="compact" (post-auto-compact rehydrate boundary
per Anthropic CC hooks contract). Emits 4 sections to stdout via
hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext per https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks
SessionStart contract:

  (a) compact_hint.json (fresh <=300s) - pre-compact handoff content
  (b) MEMORY.md head 3K chars - Karpathy section 5 Layer-2 index
  (c) last-3 close-syntheses by mtime - Karpathy section 5 Layer-3 compiled wiki
  (d) git log -5 --oneline - recent commit chronology (Layer-1 chronological proxy)

Bounded <=9500 chars total (Anthropic CC 10K stdout cap safety margin).

Distinct from existing SessionStart matcher=="*" entries (session-lifecycle-hook,
context-mode-cache-heal, cli_path_audit) - this one ONLY fires on matcher=="compact"
to avoid noise on startup/resume events.

Sister-rule integration:
- sessionstart-preload-discipline.md section The contract (3-layer preload sequence)
- auto-compact-discipline.md Rank #5 (Karpathy section 5 3-layer progressive disclosure)
- karpathy-adapted.md section 5 Wiki Compounding Surface (Layer 1/2/3 mapping)
- fm20-path-drift-cascade.md (stale-belief-propagation defense at session-resume)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as _dt
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

REPO_ROOT = Path("Z:/claude-sota-installed")
STATE_DIR = REPO_ROOT / ".claude" / "state"
HINT_PATH = STATE_DIR / "compact_hint.json"
MEMORY_INDEX = REPO_ROOT / ".claude" / "projects" / "Z--claude-sota-installed" / "memory" / "MEMORY.md"
TMP_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "tmp"
TELEMETRY_PATH = STATE_DIR / "sessionstart_compact_hint_reader.jsonl"

HINT_FRESH_SEC = 300       # <=300s sidecar freshness window
MEMORY_HEAD_CHARS = 3000   # Karpathy section 5 Layer-2 head portion
STDOUT_CAP_CHARS = 9500    # Anthropic 10K cap safety margin
GIT_LOG_LINES = 5          # recent-commit chronology


def _now_iso() -> str:
    return _dt.datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z"


def _read_stdin_payload() -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        raw = sys.stdin.read() or ""
        return json.loads(raw) if raw.strip() else {}
    except Exception:
        return {}


def _section_a_hint() -> str:
    """Section A: compact_hint.json fresh <=300s."""
    if not HINT_PATH.exists():
        return "## (a) compact_hint.json - ABSENT (no pre-compact handoff written)"
    try:
        age_sec = _dt.datetime.now().timestamp() - HINT_PATH.stat().st_mtime
        if age_sec > HINT_FRESH_SEC:
            return f"## (a) compact_hint.json - STALE ({age_sec:.0f}s > {HINT_FRESH_SEC}s cap)"
        with HINT_PATH.open(encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            data = json.load(fh)
        if not isinstance(data, dict) or data.get("version") != 1:
            return "## (a) compact_hint.json - INVALID schema"
        lines = [
            "## (a) compact_hint.json (fresh, version=1)",
            f"  ts: {data.get('ts')}",
            f"  current_goal: {data.get('current_goal')}",
            f"  test_state: {data.get('test_state')}",
            f"  next_action: {data.get('next_action')}",
            "  files_touched:",
        ]
        for f in (data.get("files_touched") or [])[:20]:
            lines.append(f"    - {f}")
        lines.append("  design_cites:")
        for c in (data.get("design_cites") or [])[:10]:
            lines.append(f"    - {c}")
        return "\n".join(lines)
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        return f"## (a) compact_hint.json - READ-ERROR: {exc!r}"


def _section_b_memory_head() -> str:
    """Section B: MEMORY.md head 3K chars (Karpathy section 5 Layer-2 index)."""
    if not MEMORY_INDEX.exists():
        return "## (b) MEMORY.md - ABSENT"
    try:
        text = MEMORY_INDEX.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        head = text[:MEMORY_HEAD_CHARS]
        return f"## (b) MEMORY.md head ({len(head)} chars):\n{head}"
    except Exception as exc:
        return f"## (b) MEMORY.md - READ-ERROR: {exc!r}"


def _section_c_close_synthesis() -> str:
    """Section C: last-3 close-syntheses by mtime (Karpathy section 5 Layer-3)."""
    patterns = [
        list(TMP_DIR.glob("wave*close-synthesis*.md")),
        list(TMP_DIR.glob("wave*-fire*-close-synthesis-*.md")),
    ]
    files: list[Path] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for grp in patterns:
        for p in grp:
            if p.name not in seen:
                files.append(p)
                seen.add(p.name)
    files.sort(key=lambda p: p.stat().st_mtime if p.exists() else 0, reverse=True)
    last3 = files[:3]
    if not last3:
        return "## (c) close-syntheses - NONE FOUND (empty Layer-3)"
    lines = [f"## (c) last-3 close-syntheses (total {len(files)}):"]
    for p in last3:
        try:
            mtime = _dt.datetime.utcfromtimestamp(p.stat().st_mtime).isoformat() + "Z"
            size = p.stat().st_size
            # Read first 400 chars summary
            preview = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")[:400]
            lines.append(f"  - {p.name} ({size} B, mtime={mtime})")
            lines.append(f"    preview: {preview[:300]}...")
        except Exception as exc:
            lines.append(f"  - {p.name} READ-ERROR: {exc!r}")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _section_d_git_log() -> str:
    """Section D: git log -5 --oneline (Layer-1 chronological proxy)."""
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "-C", str(REPO_ROOT), "log", f"-{GIT_LOG_LINES}", "--oneline"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3,
        )
        if result.returncode == 0:
            return f"## (d) git log -{GIT_LOG_LINES} --oneline:\n{result.stdout.strip()}"
        return f"## (d) git log - FAILED (rc={result.returncode}): {result.stderr.strip()[:200]}"
    except (FileNotFoundError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
        return f"## (d) git log - ERROR: {exc!r}"


def _append_telemetry(record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    try:
        STATE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with TELEMETRY_PATH.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write(json.dumps(record) + "\n")
    except Exception as exc:
        sys.stderr.write(f"[sessionstart_compact_hint_reader] telemetry failed: {exc!r}\n")


def main() -> int:
    payload = _read_stdin_payload()
    source = payload.get("source", "unknown")

    # Fire ONLY on matcher=="compact" - verified via payload.source per Anthropic CC contract
    # SessionStart payload.source in {startup, resume, clear, compact}
    if source != "compact":
        # No-op for non-compact SessionStart events
        _append_telemetry({
            "schema": "sessionstart_compact_hint_reader.v1",
            "ts": _now_iso(),
            "wire_event": "SessionStart",
            "source": source,
            "fired": False,
            "reason": "source!=compact",
        })
        return 0

    # Build 4 sections
    body_parts = [
        "POST-AUTO-COMPACT REHYDRATE (W173 P1(a) compact-matcher reader)",
        "",
        _section_a_hint(),
        "",
        _section_b_memory_head(),
        "",
        _section_c_close_synthesis(),
        "",
        _section_d_git_log(),
    ]
    body = "\n".join(body_parts)

    # Cap to <=9500 chars
    if len(body) > STDOUT_CAP_CHARS:
        body = body[:STDOUT_CAP_CHARS - 30] + "\n... [TRUNCATED at 9500 chars]"

    # Emit via hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext per Anthropic CC contract
    sys.stdout.write(json.dumps({
        "hookSpecificOutput": {
            "hookEventName": "SessionStart",
            "additionalContext": body,
        }
    }))

    _append_telemetry({
        "schema": "sessionstart_compact_hint_reader.v1",
        "ts": _now_iso(),
        "wire_event": "SessionStart",
        "source": source,
        "fired": True,
        "session_id": payload.get("session_id"),
        "agent_id": payload.get("agent_id"),
        "agent_type": payload.get("agent_type"),
        "model": payload.get("model"),
        "transcript_path": payload.get("transcript_path"),
        "body_chars": len(body),
    })

    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

# Auto-compact discipline — SOTA token-efficiency recipe for long-arc /loop sessions

# Cite-class lattice (per Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/citation-discipline.md rule #8 MIN_PRECEDENCE):
# constituents=[
#   TIER-1-DIRECT @ Karpathy §5 Wiki Compounding Surface at Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/karpathy-adapted.md §5 (LLM Wiki 3-layer compounding-surface; named-author Karpathy quote at AI Engineer fireside chat 2026-05-02 + CCBP karpathy video transcript:153 @ HEAD 64fffd53),
#   TIER-1-DIRECT @ context-mode MCP plugin v1.0.111 + v1.0.124 (ctx_batch_execute / ctx_execute_file / ctx_fetch_and_index — primary token-savings primitive measured ~98% reduction vs raw Read+Bash+WebFetch on this fire's probes),
#   TIER-1-DIRECT @ repomix README pack-grep workflow at Z:/repos/deps/repomix/README.md:979 + :1089 @ HEAD 7dfd2b96 (tree-sitter compression ~70% token reduction),
#   TIER-1-DIRECT @ coordination.md §12 rewind-first vs correct-layered at Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/coordination.md §12 (Thariq 2026-04-16 named-T2 endorsement),
#   TIER-1-DIRECT @ deepagents middleware summarization at Z:/repos/deps/deepagents/libs/deepagents/deepagents/middleware/summarization.py:122-149 @ HEAD 95f845d29745ece957144d045849f02c667ac711 (LangChain-org TruncateArgsSettings pre-emptive arg-truncation),
#   TIER-3-LOCAL-OBSERVED @ /goal directive 2026-05-13 user-trigger + 43%-at-session-start fresh probe (this fire is itself the n=1 trigger)
# ]; effective_tier=TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION per rule #8 MIN_PRECEDENCE.
#
# Sister-rule integration:
# - karpathy-adapted.md §5 Wiki Compounding Surface (Layer 1 JSONL / Layer 2 index / Layer 3 compiled wiki)
# - coordination.md §12 rewind-first
# - team-orch-patterns.md §Context Budget per agent + §1M context calibration (rot threshold ~300-400k on Opus 4.7)
# - synthesis-layer-verify.md (orchestrator-side OVER/UNDER/HNF on agent returns)
# - codex-t1-fix-forward-pattern.md §Pattern B (HNF disposition when codex T1 timeout; ranking from this fire's [B] partial trace)

## The rule

When working in long-arc /loop sessions (≥4h wall-clock OR multi-Bundle ship cycles OR cron-driven autonomous arcs), context bloat dominates token cost. The CC-runtime autocompact at ~80% threshold fires blind and produces lossy summaries (per `karpathy-adapted.md §5`). SOTA auto-compact discipline is **pre-emptive, ranked, and routes-around the autocompact mechanism entirely**.

## Ranked recipe (codex T1 ranking from /goal P2[B] partial trace 2026-05-13)

### Rank #1 — context-mode `ctx_batch_execute` (PRIMARY, ~98% savings)

**When**: any command/probe/script producing >20 lines of output. Multi-command batches with multiple search queries.

**How**:
```
mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_batch_execute(
  commands: [{label, command}, ...],
  queries: [<5-8 specific questions>],
  concurrency: 4-8 for I/O-bound batches
)
```

Raw stdout/output indexed into FTS5 in subprocess sandbox; only the search-result excerpts enter context. **Single batch_execute call replaces 30+ ctx_execute + 10+ ctx_search calls**.

**Sub-variants**:
- `ctx_execute(language: shell|python|javascript, code, intent: "...")` — single command with intent → indexed if output >5KB, returns titled previews
- `ctx_execute_file(path, language, code)` — read+process file content in sandbox; only printed result enters context
- `ctx_fetch_and_index(requests: [{url, source}], concurrency: 4-8)` — replaces WebFetch; raw HTML stays in sandbox, ~3KB preview + searchable knowledge base

### Rank #2 — repomix pack→grep workflow (~70% compression for repo-scope audits)

**When**: line-by-line auditing a `Z:/repos/deps/<repo>` subtree spanning ≥5 files. Replaces 10+ Read calls.

**How** (per `Z:/repos/deps/repomix/src/mcp/mcpServer.ts:20-23 @ HEAD 7dfd2b96`):
1. **Pack**: `mcp__repomix__pack_codebase(directory, compress=true)` → returns `outputId`. Tree-sitter compression ~70% per `Z:/repos/deps/repomix/README.md:1089 @ 7dfd2b96`.
2. **Grep**: `mcp__repomix__grep_repomix_output(outputId, pattern, contextLines=5)` for incremental retrieval — preferred over re-packing uncompressed per README:979.
3. **Skill persist** (if learning is durably reusable): **DO NOT** use `mcp__repomix__generate_skill(directory, skillName)` for routine auto-compact reuse — that primitive writes `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` outside the documented install/provenance path, violating cardinal-rule-5 install-priority + cardinal-rule-6 official-native-channel. Instead: (a) persist topic-scoped findings to `.claude/projects/*/memory/` per Karpathy §5 Layer-3 compiled wiki (no install-class artifact created), OR (b) escalate as install-class candidate via sota-researcher dispatch with convergence-gate Axis-1+2+3 verification + Section 14 marketplace install path + `docs/install-provenance.md` row. Local `generate_skill` invocations bypass T1+T2 audit and provenance log.

### Rank #3 — Pre-emptive `/compact <hint>` (superpowers + Karpathy §5 layered)

**When**: context approaches 300k tokens (rot threshold on Opus 4.7 per Thariq 2026-04-16 at `coordination.md §12 Rewind-first` row Mid-task hint). **NEVER let CC autocompact fire blind at 80%**.

**How**: invoke `/compact` with a focused hint that steers the lossy summary toward retained-value content:
```
/compact focus on Bundle ship cumulative state and active queue, drop verbose codex T1 verdict bodies
```

Per `coordination.md §12` row 2: hint steered summary BEFORE rot dominates. Karpathy §5 Layer-3 compiled wiki is the equivalent persistent-storage discipline.

### Rank #3.5 — PreCompact hook layer (4-layer incumbent stack)

**When**: `/compact` manual OR auto triggers the PreCompact preservation stack; ECC suggest-compact is the adjacent PreToolUse Edit|Write threshold-suggestion layer (NOT PreCompact-class). Matching hooks run in parallel per Anthropic CC hooks contract.

| Layer | Plugin | Surface | Event | LOC |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Prompt-augmentation | fcakyon/intelligent-compact | 6-section priority injection (A-F) | PreCompact | 71 |
| State-save audit | ECC pre-compact | compaction-log.txt timestamp | PreCompact | 48 |
| Session-event SQLite | context-mode precompact.mjs | <2KB XML resume-snapshot | PreCompact | 76 |
| Tool-call threshold | ECC suggest-compact | `/compact` suggestion at N=50 + every 25 | PreToolUse Edit\|Write | 80 |

**CRITICAL**: per CC v2.1.105+, PreCompact `{"decision":"block"}` OR exit 2 BLOCKS compaction — reserve for hard-block scenarios. All 4 layers above currently exit 0 / emit no block decision = SAFE (advisory-only). suggest-compact is threshold-adjacent (different event surface), not part of the PreCompact 3-layer preservation stack.

Cite classes (per `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/citation-discipline.md` rule #8):

## Supporting disciplines (Rank #4-#7)

### Rank #4 — Rewind-first on failure (coordination.md §12)

When an approach fails mid-iteration, prefer `/rewind` to last sound state over layering corrections. Drops failed-tail + retains good reads. Per Thariq table at `coordination.md §12`:

| Situation | Use | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Explored N files, tried approach A, failed | **Rewind** to after-reads, re-prompt with learning | Keep expensive reads, drop failed attempt |
| Session bloated mid-task | `/compact <hint>` | Steered lossy summary |
| Genuinely new task | `/clear` + brief | Zero rot, controlled carry-over |
| High-volume exploration, only conclusion matters | Subagent fork | Tool noise GC'd on exit |

### Rank #5 — Karpathy §5 3-layer progressive disclosure

Per `karpathy-adapted.md §5 Wiki Compounding Surface`:
- **Layer 1 chronological log**: `.claude/state/*.jsonl` (codex_review_HEAD_*.jsonl / codex_consult_*_OUT.txt / mcp_health.jsonl / subagent_transcripts.jsonl)
- **Layer 2 index**: `.claude/projects/*/memory/MEMORY.md` (≤200 lines / 150-char one-line topic pointers)
- **Layer 3 compiled wiki**: `docs/karpathy-llm-wiki-practice.md` + `.claude/rules/*.md` + per-topic `memory/` files

**MEMORY.md discipline**: NEVER write content directly; always one-line pointer with `[Title](file.md) — hook` format. If a top entry exceeds ~3000 chars, archive immediately (this rule's codification trigger).

### Rank #6 — Pre-emptive arg truncation (deepagents pattern, doc-only port)

Per `Z:/repos/deps/deepagents/.../summarization.py:122-149 @ 95f845d2`: TruncateArgsSettings TypedDict — for long-arc /loop fires touching Edit/Write/execute args with content >4KB, retain only first ~20-1000 chars of the arg with a `"[truncated — N chars; see file/commit for full content]"` placeholder. NOT active middleware in claude-sota-installed (no Python SDK runtime) — but discipline applies operationally: when args bloat, persist the full content to disk first (Write tool), then reference path in subsequent steps.

### Rank #7 — Subagent forks for high-output exploration

Per `parallel-agent-wave.md §Fork-vs-fresh routing` + `team-orch-patterns.md §1M context calibration`: dispatch tool-noisy exploration into subagent fork (forked = inherits parent context, conversation-history-cached) OR fresh subagent (zero parent context, isolation). The subagent's tool output GC'd on exit — only the final return enters parent context.

## How to apply (operational checklist)

For every long-arc /loop fire:

1. **At session start**: check `[████░░░] N/1000k` context indicator. If >20% at start, immediately invoke `/compact <hint>` BEFORE any work
2. **For every >20-line probe**: route through `ctx_batch_execute` (multi-command + queries) OR `ctx_execute` (single command + intent)
3. **For every repo-scope audit ≥5 files**: pack with repomix BEFORE Reading individual files
4. **For every URL fetch**: prefer `ctx_fetch_and_index` over `WebFetch` (raw HTML stays in sandbox)
5. **At ~250k context**: invoke `/compact <hint>` pre-emptively (NOT wait for autocompact at 80%)
6. **At ~300k context**: STOP planning new work; ship current Bundle + commit + `/clear` between Bundle cycles
7. **On approach-failure**: rewind to last-sound-state with `/rewind`, NEVER layer corrections
8. **MEMORY.md hygiene**: if any entry exceeds ~300 chars or grows beyond a single line of pointer text, immediately archive + reset to proper one-line index format

## Anti-patterns

- **Let autocompact fire blind at 80%** — refuted by `karpathy-adapted.md §5` + Thariq tip 2026-04-16. Autocompact at the model's "least intelligent point" produces lossy summaries with no operator steering. Pre-emptive `/compact <hint>` is the SOTA.
- **Read large files into context for analysis** — refuted by file_writing_policy + ctx_execute discipline. Use `ctx_execute_file(path, code)` to process in sandbox; only printed answer enters context.
- **Bash for >20-line commands** — refuted by PreToolUse hook guidance. Route through `ctx_execute(language: shell)` or `ctx_batch_execute`.
- **Multiple ctx_execute calls when one batch suffices** — refuted by ctx_batch_execute description. One call replaces 30+ ctx_execute + 10+ ctx_search calls.
- **Repeated Read of same large file** — refuted by progressive disclosure. Read once, persist findings to a topic-scoped memory file, reference from there.
- **MEMORY.md content-as-entries** (writing content directly into the index) — refuted by Karpathy §5 Layer-2 vs Layer-3 distinction. Index = pointers; content = linked files.
- **Subagent for sub-1000-token operations** — refuted by `parallel-agent-wave.md §Fork-vs-fresh`. Subagent overhead > savings for trivial ops.

## When NOT to apply

- **Short sessions (<10 turns total)**: discipline overhead exceeds savings
- **Interactive debugging single-file edits**: `/compact` interrupts flow; routine Edit+Read suffices
- **Hot-path tool calls where every ms counts**: ctx_execute subprocess startup adds ~50-200ms; not worth on single-line `git status`
- **Files <100 lines or outputs <20 lines**: just use Read/Bash directly
